Looking at the checkpoint timing, the best model was saved at the end of training through HuggingFace's `load_best_model_at_end=True` mechanism, which should correspond to the earliest checkpoint with the lowest eval loss at step 1000 (0.1717). Since training resumed from checkpoint-600 and the first visible eval is at step 1000, that's likely where the best model checkpoint was created.

Since the config mentions resuming from checkpoint-600 but I don't see it in the output, it was likely consumed during training. The eval loss spike from 0.17 to 0.48 is still concerning though, so let me try to recover those truncated middle evaluation points to understand the full trajectory.
